Motivation: 现有基于生成的推荐方法（GR）在处理多模态数据时，通常依赖物品级的配对观察，导致标记化受限于可用模态的交集；且引入非配对观察因表示微小偏移跨越量化边界而产生不兼容的标识符序列，难以处理。

Method: 提出UnpairGR模型：1) 从配对、仅图像和仅文本观察中学习统一的语义-ID空间；2) 模态特定处理仅限于轻量级输入投影，后续Transformer和残差码本在所有观察条件下共享；3) 配对观察建立可靠性引导的跨模态共识，单模态观察直接细化相同表示和代码；4) 固定学习到的tokenizer为自回归推荐器提供静态目标，无需特征插补或模态特定码本。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，UnpairGR在全观测和不完整观测设置下均能一致地提高推荐性能。

Conclusion: UnpairGR有效解决了多模态生成推荐中非配对数据的利用难题，通过统一语义空间和共享架构提升了模型的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation over discrete semantic identifiers (IDs). Although recent multimodal GR methods improve semantic ID construction with visual and textual information, they typically require item-level paired observations, restricting tokenization to the intersection of modality availability. Moreover, incorporating unpaired observations is nontrivial because small representation shifts may cross quantization boundaries and produce incompatible identifier sequences. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{Unpair}ed Modality-Agnostic \textbf{G}enerative \textbf{R}ecommendation (UnpairGR), which learns a unified semantic-ID space from paired, image-only, and text-only observations. UnpairGR confines modality-specific processing to lightweight input projections while sharing the subsequent Transformer and residual codebooks across all observation conditions. Paired observations establish a reliability-guided cross-modal consensus, whereas unimodal observations directly refine the same representations and codes. The learned tokenizer is then fixed to provide stationary targets for a single autoregressive recommender, without feature imputation, modality-specific codebooks, or fallback mappings. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that UnpairGR consistently improves recommendation performance under both fully observed and incomplete-observation settings.

</details>


### [246] [Requirement--Evidence Alignment for Compositional E-Commerce Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02500)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Yuqin Lan,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出REAlign框架，通过显式连接查询需求与可见证据，解决电商复合查询重排序中约束被忽略的问题，显著提升排名质量并减少违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排序器将复合查询的多重要求坍缩为聚合相关性得分，常导致满足表面主题相似但不可行的产品（near misses）被错误推荐，无法有效处理联合约束。

Method: 引入REAlign框架：1) 明确区分满足、违反和支持不足的条件；2) 构建针对需求的对比样本以暴露失败模式；3) 使用需求感知的组相对策略优化（Requirement-Aware Group-Relative Policy Optimization）进行无重复部分排名优化；4) 列表效用函数兼顾相关性、需求满足度、证据支持及输出有效性。

Result: 在两个固定池电商基准测试中，相比强监督及策略优化基线，在相同训练预算下取得一致改进；顶级候选者违规更少，浅层排名增益更大；消融实验验证了需求建模、证据 grounding 和分解优化的互补价值。

Conclusion: REAlign通过显式对齐需求与证据，有效解决了电商复合查询中的约束满足问题，提升了重排序的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Compositional e-commerce queries express multiple requirements that must hold jointly, yet existing rerankers collapse these constraints into aggregate relevance and often promote topical near misses over feasible products. In this paper, we introduce REAlign, a novel requirement-evidence-aligned reranking framework that explicitly connects typed query requirements with visible evidence. REAlign distinguishes satisfied, violated, and unsupported conditions, constructs requirement-targeted contrasts that expose failure modes, and optimizes duplicate-free partial rankings through Requirement-Aware Group-Relative Policy Optimization. Its list utility preserves relevance while incorporating requirement satisfaction, evidence support, material violations, and output validity. Experiments on two fixed-pool e-commerce benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong supervised and policy-optimization baselines under matched training budgets, with fewer violations among top-ranked candidates and larger gains at shallow ranks. Controlled ablations confirm the complementary value of requirement modeling, evidence grounding, and decomposed optimization.

</details>


### [247] [Between-User Collapse Under Popularity-Biased Feedback: A Centered-Covariance Theorem and Computable Phase Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02548)
*Sahil Medepalli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了流行度偏差的BPR训练如何重塑协同过滤嵌入的用户间几何结构。通过均值中心化用户协方差矩阵C，证明了在流行度偏差反馈下，C会收敛到与物品噪声协方差成正比的稳态，导致用户区分度下降（收缩）。推导出了超参数间的相边界公式，并在MovieLens-25M上验证了理论预测。然而，在实际部署规模的正则化强度下，这种收缩效应微小且不影响推荐指标；仅在损害推荐质量的强正则化下才显著。基于理论的恢复干预未能提升效果。该相边界可由训练好的模型直接计算，帮助从业者判断系统是否处于强收缩 regime，实验表明可部署设置通常远离该危险区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多使用未中心化的二阶矩分析协同过滤，但无法准确衡量用户间的可区分性。此外，业界普遍关注推荐准确率，却缺乏对嵌入空间几何结构（特别是用户间距离/相关性）因流行度偏差而恶化的深入理解及量化评估方法。

Method: 1. 定义均值中心化用户协方差矩阵 $C=\tfrac1n U^\top H U$ 作为衡量用户可区分性的指标。
2. 在流行度偏差反馈和静态物品的假设下，证明 $C$ 收敛至与物品噪声协方差 $Q$ 成正比的稳态。
3. 推导训练超参数 $(α,λ_{neg},γ,d)$ 之间的闭合形式相边界，用于区分嵌入空间的“收缩”与“扩张”。
4. 在 MovieLens-25M 数据集上进行实验验证理论预测的方向性。
5. 评估不同正则化强度下的实际影响及基于理论的恢复干预效果。

Result: 1. 理论上：证明了流行度偏差导致用户间几何结构向噪声基底收缩。
2. 实证上：验证了方向性预测正确。
3. 实际影响上：在部署规模的正则化下，收缩效应真实存在但极小，未反映在推荐的任何质量指标中。
4. 阈值效应：$\alpha$ 驱动的各向异性收缩仅在严重损害推荐器性能的高正则化强度下发生。
5. 干预效果：基于理论提出的部署时恢复干预未能改善推荐质量。
6. 实用性：相边界可通过已训练模型的嵌入、物品交互计数和超参数直接计算，无需模拟反馈回路。实验显示可部署设置位于强收缩区域之外。

Conclusion: 流行度偏差确实会导致协同过滤嵌入中用户间几何结构的收缩，但在常规部署配置下，这种效应对最终推荐质量的影响微乎其微。虽然理论推导出的相边界可用于监控潜在风险，但当前实践中的安全设置远离危险的强收缩区域。简单的基于理论的恢复手段并不能解决或改善这一问题，表明在标准BPR训练中，几何结构的细微变化并未主导推荐性能。

Abstract: We study how popularity-biased BPR training reshapes the between-user geometry of collaborative-filtering embeddings. We work with the mean-centered user covariance $C=\tfrac1n U^\top H U$, the object that measures how distinguishable users are from one another, as opposed to the uncentered second moment used in prior work. We prove that under popularity-biased feedback with stationary items, $C$ converges to a steady state proportional to the item-noise covariance $Q$. Thus between-user spread collapses toward a noise floor. We derive a closed-form, computable phase boundary in the training hyperparameters $(α,λ_{neg},γ,d)$ separating contraction from expansion, and validate both directional predictions on MovieLens-25M. We then examine the limits of the effect. At deployment-scale regularization the predicted contraction is real and policy-driven but small, and it is not reflected in any recommendation-level metric we measured. The $α$-driven anisotropic-collapse mechanism operates only at regularization strengths that degrade the recommender. A deployment-time restoration intervention derived from the theory does not improve recommendation quality. The boundary is computable from a trained model's embeddings, item interaction counts, and training hyperparameters, so a practitioner can check whether a deployed system sits in the strong-collapse regime without simulating the feedback loop. In our experiments the boundary places deployable settings far from that regime.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond the Final Prompt: Measuring the Effect of Within-Conversation Context on AI Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02556)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究证实，在多轮对话中忽略上下文会导致近半数（44.7%）的AI回答发生实质性改变；虽然压缩前文能显著减少差异（降至30.8%），但无法完全替代完整对话语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在AI系统评测中，仅将最终用户消息视为查询而忽略多轮对话历史，是否会导致答案出现实质性偏差。

Method: 从商业语料库和PRISM数据集选取180个英语多轮对话，保持最终用户消息和目标答案模型不变，分别生成基于完整对话、仅最终消息、以及最终消息加160字压缩前文的三种回答，并由裁判模型评估其差异及请求满足度。

Result: 完整对话与孤立最终消息的回答在44.7%的情况下存在实质性差异，前者满意度评分高出0.49分；添加压缩前文可将实质性差异率降低至30.8%，满意度差距缩小至0.01分，但仍无法完全消除差异。

Conclusion: 对话历史对AI回答质量有显著影响；压缩上下文虽能缓解但未根除因缺失信息导致的偏差，表明在处理多轮对话时保留完整或近似完整的语境至关重要。

Abstract: An isolated final user message is often treated as the query in evaluations of AI systems. In a conversation, however, the actionable request may be distributed across preceding turns. We directly test whether that omitted within-conversation context changes answers. For each of 180 English multi-turn conversations sampled from a governed commercial corpus and the public PRISM dataset, we hold the final user message and requested answer model constant while generating three answers: one from the full role-labelled conversation, one from the final message alone, and one from the final message plus a prefix-only reconstruction capped at 160 words. A separately requested judge model evaluates answers under randomized labels. The prespecified primary endpoint is a material difference that could change what the user does, rather than a difference in style or detail. After inverse-probability weighting to the eligible cohorts, the full-conversation and isolated-final answers differ materially in 44.7% of cases (95% bootstrap CI 33.8% to 56.1%). Full-conversation answers score 0.49 points higher on a 0 to 4 request-satisfaction scale (0.32 to 0.67). Adding the compressed prefix reduces the material-difference rate to 30.8% (20.2% to 42.1%), a 13.9-point reduction (4.9% to 24.1%), and reduces the mean satisfaction gap to 0.01 points (-0.12 to 0.13). Yet compression is not equivalent to the complete dialogue context: almost one third of answers remain materially different. An order-swapped repeat on 48 cases yields 91.7% agreement and kappa = 0.83 for the primary decision. The study concerns preceding turns in the same conversation and does not test persistent memory across separate conversations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过守恒-同构编码（CCE）框架，重新审视了莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试和中文房间等经典思想实验。研究区分了“行为表现”与“内部结构效率”，指出尽管无压缩查找系统和紧凑生成系统可能达到相似的行为成功率，但后者因复用内部结构而具有更高的操作意识（$\kappa_T$）。这一区分有助于从AI安全角度理解“理解”的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在重新审视关于人工智能中“理解”的经典哲学争论（如莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试、中文房间），通过引入守恒-同构编码（CCE）框架，将外在行为表现与支撑该行为的内部组织结构分离开来，从而为后续的AI安全性分析提供理论动机。

Method: 构建了一个形式化的玩具符号模型，在该模型中：1. 使用任务性能（$W_{causal,T}$）来衡量成功行为；2. 使用操作意识（$\kappa_T$）来衡量保留的内部结构对行为的支持效率。对比了两种系统：依赖未重用映射存储的“无压缩查找系统”和复用紧凑内部结构的“紧凑生成系统”。

Result: 在相同的设定下，无压缩查找系统和紧凑生成系统原则上可以实现可比拟的行为成功度（$W_{causal,T}$），但在操作意识（$\kappa_T$）上存在显著差异。前者依赖于不断扩张且未被重用的映射存储，效率较低；后者通过复用紧凑的内部结构支持行为，效率较高。

Conclusion: 研究通过将外在表现与支持它的组织方式分离，重构了关于“理解”的经典争论。这种区分不仅澄清了行为能力与内部结构效率之间的差异，还强调了这种差异对于未来AI安全性分析的重要性，表明仅关注行为表现不足以评估系统的内在机制或安全性。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [250] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的自进化智能体，能够仅通过自然语言指令自动生成、评估、运行并优化OpenFOAM流体模拟，旨在降低CFD使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决使用OpenFOAM等开源求解器时需要深厚专业知识、技能以及耗时配置文件的痛点，减轻用户负担。

Method: 以Qwen-coder 2.5-14B为预训练基础，在针对7种求解器、13种网格模板及y-plus感知数值策略的252个文本提示上进行微调；核心算法包含一个由7个阶段组成的复杂进化循环，并采用检索增强生成（RAG）重试上下文、字典级手术修补和提示多样性改写三种防退化机制来防止自我训练过程中的模型崩溃。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够自主完成从创建到演化整个OpenFOAM模拟流程的智能系统，实现了生成式AI与严格流体模拟的结合。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM加速了快速原型设计过程，并通过自动化复杂配置工作流，使高级计算流体动力学（CFD）技术更加普及化（民主化）。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [251] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对中小企业（SMEs）在使用大型语言模型（LLMs）时面临的幻觉问题，提出并评估了VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成（RAG）方法。实验表明，结合外部知识源的RAG技术能显著降低幻觉和 misinformation，提升响应质量与决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在中小企业中的应用日益广泛，但其生成的输出常存在“幻觉”现象，导致信息误导，降低了用户对模型可靠性和信任度的信心。

Method: 提出了VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种建模方法来缓解幻觉和信息风险；使用LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等主流LLM进行实验评估，从有用性、幻觉风险、上下文相关性以及人类可解释性四个维度衡量性能。

Result: RAG增强的LLMs显著提高了响应质量，有效减少了幻觉和信息错误，从而支持更可靠、可信且具备上下文感知能力的决策过程。

Conclusion: 引入外部知识源的RAG方法是解决中小企业环境中LLM幻觉问题的有效途径，能够显著提升模型输出的可信度与支持业务决策的能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [252] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对本地部署大语言模型（LLM）在消费级硬件上的能耗问题，构建了基于RTX 4060Ti GPU的可复现硬件级能源基准测试。通过对9个开源LLM（1B-7B参数）在Ollama引擎下的运行进行采样，发现模型架构和量化策略对能效的影响远超参数量本身，其中gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能效最优，而qwen3.5:2b因内部推理机制表现出异常高的单次提示能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着隐私保护和本地推理需求的增加，LLM的本地部署日益普及。然而，现有基准测试主要关注准确性，缺乏对消费级硬件上实际能源消耗的详细表征，导致用户难以评估不同模型在能效方面的表现。

Method: 研究使用Ollama推理引擎，在单张消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上执行9个开源LLM（参数规模1B至7B）。通过nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，针对固定提示集评估平均/峰值功率、每提示总能耗（J/prompt）、每输出令牌能耗（J/token）以及吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: 1. 能效不仅取决于参数量，还受模型架构和量化策略显著影响。
2. gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b表现最佳，分别达到最低能耗（0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token）和最高吞吐量（>170 tok/s）。
3. 7B-Mistral模型的每令牌能耗比最高效模型高出多达4.4倍。
4. qwen3.5:2b因扩展的内部推理过程，导致单次提示能耗异常高，揭示了区分生成模式对效率指标的重要性。

Conclusion: 在评估本地部署LLM时，不能仅依据参数量判断性能，必须综合考虑架构、量化及推理模式对能耗的影响。建议在未来的效率评估中，明确区分不同的令牌生成模式，以更准确地反映实际应用场景中的能源成本。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [253] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，通过让LLM生成零样本思维链（CoT）轨迹并投影到潜在空间，基于内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度来选择核心问题集，从而在大幅降低评估成本的同时保持高分辨率分数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型（LLM）评估计算开销巨大；现有的核心集选择方法存在严重瓶颈：要么需要大量历史日志（如项目反应理论），要么受限于表面词汇偏差，无法捕捉任务背后的推理流形。

Method: 提出无需训练的CoT-Core框架。利用LLM展开零样本思维链（CoT）推理轨迹，将这些路径投影到潜在空间中，根据内在逻辑等价性对问题进行聚类，而非依赖表层文本相似性。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上的广泛实验表明，CoT-Core能显著降低评估成本，同时保持高保真度的分数估计。研究还揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件，证明其有效性本质上受任务复杂度的制约。

Conclusion: 基于逻辑等价性的核心问题选择能有效解决冷启动和词汇偏差问题，为高效评估LLM提供了新范式，但其效果取决于任务的复杂性。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [254] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的管道，利用合成优化问题数据集提升大语言模型（LLM）在组合优化建模中的性能。实验显示，该方法无需微调即可显著提高Qwen 3 30B模型的求解准确率，为低成本部署LLM决策支持工具提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管优化建模在物流、医疗和供应链管理中至关重要，但现有大语言模型在处理组合优化问题时，常因缺乏领域专业知识而产生结构不一致或不完整的公式化结果。当前亟需解决如何有效利用LLM进行准确、结构化建模的问题。

Method: 构建了一个基于检索增强生成的管道：1) 使用Text2Zinc种子描述和LLM创建的专业角色，合成了500个带有JSON格式说明及验证过的Python求解器脚本的优化问题；2) 将这些数据编码至Chroma向量数据库；3) 对于推理问题，通过语义相似度检索相关示例作为上下文引导LangChain LLM代理；4) 在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上，使用NL4OPT、MAMO Easy和MAMO Complex三个基准测试集进行评估。

Result: 引入语义验证的合成示例后，模型性能显著提升：NL4OPT准确率从40%升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%升至56%。合成数据集结合检索增强显著提高了求解准确性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 将合成数据集生成与检索增强相结合，提供了一种有效的替代微调的方案。这表明，结合领域特定的合成语料库与检索增强技术，可以在无需高昂模型重训练成本的情况下，成为在实际决策支持场景中部署基于LLM的优化工具的实用途径。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [255] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于记忆的LLM自改进代理中存在的“回声差距”（Echo Gap）问题，即模型倾向于重用被高估的错误记忆。通过提出误差独立性假设（EIA）并实现去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD基准测试中将执行准确率提升至56.9%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM自改进代理依赖外部存储和评分来指导后续行为，但在部署环境中缺乏真实标签，导致评分仅由LLM自身评估产生。这种机制容易引发错误补偿效应，使得代理无法识别被高估的记忆，从而重复犯错。

Method: 1. 理论分析：形式化定义“误差独立性假设”（EIA），证明其是纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，即可用信号必须追踪真相且与记忆偏差解耦。2. 算法设计：提出无答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，用于修正存储奖励中的偏差。3. 实验验证：在BIRD文本到SQL基准上进行端到端测试。

Result: 1. 发现“回声差距”现象：不正确的片段获得 inflated rewards（膨胀奖励），导致代理优先重用其最自信的错误。2. LUCID算法表现优异：将执行准确率提升至56.9%。3. 对比优势：比Memento风格的自评分代理高出2.9个百分点（平均种子增益），比同架构无记忆代理高出4.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 仅依靠LLM自我评分会导致严重的偏差累积，必须满足误差独立性假设才能有效校正。LUCID算法通过解决这一根本问题，实现了自改进代理性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [256] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JouleShare框架，通过离线精确计算Shapley能量建立请求级能耗基准，并利用轻量级校准模型JCalib实现低成本、公平的在线请求级能耗分配，解决了现有方法在批量LLM服务中无法准确衡量单请求能耗的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM服务提高了吞吐量但使能耗核算复杂化。现有的GPU功率遥测数据是聚合的，而可持续报告、成本分摊和工作负载分析需要请求级别的能耗数据。现有的基准和研究要么仅提供模型/阶段/令牌级别的能耗，要么仅从概念上引用Shapley公平性，缺乏实测的请求级真实值，导致实践中使用的分配规则与公平分配的偏差未知。

Method: 1. 构建离线基准测试工具：使用vLLM重放请求子集，集成GPU功率遥测数据，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量作为地面真值（Ground Truth）。2. 开发轻量级校准模型JCalib：学习从低成本的请求特征预测Shapley份额，以便在服务时实时使用。3. 评估不同批量策略（静态和连续）下的归因误差，并对比Token比例分配、采样Shapley等方法。

Result: 1. Token比例分配与精确Shapley存在显著差距：静态批量下平均归一化L1误差为0.440，连续批量下为0.458，且该差距在三款数据中心GPU上复现。2. JCalib显著降低误差：静态批量下降至0.116，连续批量下降至0.177，优于不可在线使用的独立测量基线，同时保持准确的批量总能耗效率。3. 采样Shapley可扩展参考组规模，但差距依然存在，单次离线校准仍是最准确的可部署规则。

Conclusion: 在批量执行环境下，基于令牌的归因不是边际能耗的可靠代理。通过测量的Shapley地面真值校准低成本请求特征，可以实现更公平的请求级能耗分配。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [257] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Motif-Mamba，通过在Mamba的线性时间状态空间模型中引入受三节点网络基序约束的低秩循环路径，增强维度间交互，解决长序列建模中的效率与表达能力平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mamba模型虽然提供了线性时间的替代方案，但其主要的对角线状态转换限制了状态维度间的显式交互，影响了长序列建模的能力。

Method: 提出Motif-Mamba，这是一种结构化的状态空间模型。它借鉴三节点网络基序的动力学特性，将隐藏状态投影到一个紧凑的动力学子空间，施加由基序引导的交互，然后将结果动力学映射回原始状态空间，从而在保持Mamba线性时间循环结构的同时增强跨维度通信。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准测试以及脑机接口解码实验中，Motif-Mamba相比Mamba骨干网络均表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 受基序引导的低秩动力学为长程序列建模提供了一种有效的结构先验，能够显著提升大语言模型在长序列任务上的表现。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [258] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova 是一个端到端的 JIT 编译器，旨在通过融合操作、优化内存层级和寄存器级调优，实现对硬件映射的绝对控制，从而在 RTX 3060 上显著提升深度学习模型的吞吐量和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高级张量框架的即时执行（eager execution）模型缺乏对整个计算图的可见性和对硬件/内存的细粒度控制，导致无法最大化物理硬件利用率。

Method: 设计 Nova 编译器，将即时执行捕获并统一为单一值语义方言，实现激进的全图优化；使用分析配置器（Analytic Configurator）基于算术强度确定性地推导最优执行调度；利用结构哈希运行时从计算结构直接合成细粒度内核。

Result: 在 RTX 3060 上，Nova 在 TF32 矩阵乘法上与 cuBLAS 和 XLA 持平或略优，相对误差 < 5e-4；模型层面吞吐量比 PyTorch 高 10.6%，比 XLA 高 4.4%；内存占用减少高达 29%，成功训练了 PyTorch 因显存溢出（OOM）而无法运行的 1.44 亿参数模型。

Conclusion: Nova 通过自动化全图优化和确定性调度，有效解决了深度学习中硬件映射效率低下的问题，在保持数值精度的同时显著提升了性能并降低了内存需求。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [259] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN是一个用于检测检索增强、智能体和记忆 grounding LLM 系统中上下文幻觉的统一工具包和交互式 Web UI，整合了三种检测范式并支持白盒/黑盒设置下的响应级和片段级检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前在检索增强生成（RAG）、智能体及记忆 grounded LLM 系统中，面对流畅但缺乏证据支持的“上下文幻觉”问题，现有检测工具往往分散且缺乏统一接口的问题。

Method: 构建了一个统一的工具和 Web UI，整合了表示探测（representation probing）、不确定性估计（uncertainty estimation）和裁判式验证（judge-style verification）三种检测范式，以及生成前查询可答性任务；支持响应级和片段级的检查，兼容白盒和黑盒设置，并提供轻量级插件设计以扩展新检测器。

Result: 实现了通过 Web UI 对用户提供的情境-查询-答案三元组进行实时分析，提供幻觉评分、不支持片段的突出显示以及检测器的侧边对比；演示了其在幻觉检测、查询可答性及长期记忆系统中的忠实度门控方面的应用。

Conclusion: SIRIN 提供了一个集成化的解决方案，有效提升了 LLM 系统在复杂场景下对上下文幻觉的检测能力和可解释性，其源代码已开源。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [260] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了视觉语言模型（VLMs）中视觉表征的动态重构机制，发现语言提示不仅能提取信息，还能通过“抽象参考表征”和“属性调制”因果性地改变视觉表征，支持目标导向任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将VLM中的视觉表征视为静态的信息仓库，缺乏对语言如何动态重塑视觉表征的深入理解，特别是关于目标导向语言如何处理视觉信息的机制尚不明确。

Method: 通过提取对比引导向量分析抽象参考表征，验证其在不同对象、任务语境及合成/自然图像中的泛化能力；检测多层网络中目标相关属性的选择性放大现象；实施因果干预以验证属性调制对模型响应分布的中介作用。

Result: 1. 识别出一种抽象参考表征，其对应的对比引导向量因果性地影响模型预测，且具有高度泛化性；2. 证明后期层会选择性放大与目标相关的视觉属性；3. 因果干预证实属性调制介导了VLM的响应分布。

Conclusion: VLMs中的跨模态处理是动态的：视觉令牌并非静态信息库，而是根据语言查询进行调制，以更好地支持下游任务。

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [261] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RagTester，一种针对RAG系统的自动化端到端测试方法，通过生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出来检测系统故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统的可靠性依赖于生成模型、嵌入模型、检索机制和提示构建策略之间的复杂交互，现有测试手段难以全面评估这种交互带来的潜在问题。

Method: RagTester采用覆盖导向的测试生成策略，针对复杂段落、无支持查询和文档覆盖标准生成测试用例；利用LLM作为裁判评估结果，并在24种配置（8个LLM与6个嵌入模型的组合）下进行大规模执行与对比实验。

Result: 在72,000次测试执行中，RagTester检测到21,633个故障，比基线测试输入生成器多出6.6%，且在24种配置中的20种表现更优。发现的故障包括检索不准确、答案无依据、未充分利用检索上下文以及解读复杂段落困难。

Conclusion: 面向覆盖率的测试生成能有效暴露检索与生成组件交互导致的故障，支持在部署前对RAG配置进行评估。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [262] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出H+ Embedding，一种通过预测可变长度短语分区来平衡检索精度与效率的多粒度检索器，在医学等术语密集型任务中显著优于全局向量方法，同时比Token级交互更节省资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索表示存在两极分化：单向量检索器过度压缩局部相关性信号，而Token级晚期交互导致高昂的索引、存储和计算成本。旨在解决这一矛盾，探索介于全局向量和Token之间的上下文依赖短语作为检索单元的有效性。

Method: 引入H+ Embedding统一多粒度检索器，该方法预测可变长度的短语分区，将未覆盖的Token保留为单例，并应用基于重要性引导的单位选择与加权MaxSim交互机制。

Result: 在16个科学、医学和多语言任务中，其短语检索分支的全局检索分支在macro nDCG@10上高出6.91；Token级交互性能接近但文档向量减少13.7%；在中低向量预算下优于内容独立的分组规则。

Conclusion: 上下文依赖的短语交互为全球压缩和Token级交互之间提供了理想的中间质量-成本平衡点，适用于实际检索系统。

Abstract: Terminology-intensive retrieval, especially in medical settings, depends on preserving multi-word entities, abbreviations, numerical constraints, and compositional concepts. However, existing representations lie at two extremes: single-vector retrievers often over-compress local relevance signals, while token-level late interaction retains every tokenizer subword at substantial indexing, storage, and scoring cost. This mismatch raises a natural question: can context-dependent phrases provide a useful retrieval unit between global vectors and tokens? We introduce H+ Embedding, a unified multi-granularity retriever that predicts variable-length phrase partitions, preserves uncovered tokens as singletons, and applies importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim interaction. Across 16 scientific, medical, and bilingual tasks, its phrase retrieval branch exceeds the global retrieval branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10. It also nearly matches Token while using 13.7% fewer document vectors and outperforms content-independent grouping rules under moderate vector budgets. Context-dependent phrase interaction therefore provides an intermediate quality-cost point between global compression and token-level interaction for practical retrieval systems.

</details>


### [263] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于GitHub Copilot的大规模生产数据，首次对AI编程代理（Agent）的工作负载进行了系统性分析。研究发现其具有稀疏的用户交互、高KV缓存命中率但跨轮次易失效、以及显著的“快速响应-长等待”特征，为构建面向Agent的原生基础设施提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务系统主要基于聊天机器人场景设计，而AI编程代理（如GitHub Copilot, Claude Code等）将多步LLM推理与工具执行交织，形成了截然不同的工作负载模式。目前缺乏针对这种新型工作负载的大规模实证研究，导致现有系统架构难以高效支持Agent应用。

Method: 使用2026年6月采样的GitHub Copilot生产级追踪数据进行大规模特性分析。数据集涵盖320万用户、1300万会话、7.61亿次LLM调用和95万亿Token。通过分析会话结构、KV缓存命中率、Token消耗分布及时间跨度，识别工作负载的关键属性，并设计了轻量级的空闲时间预测器以优化资源调度。

Result: 1. **会话结构**：由稀疏的用户发起轮次组成，每轮包含几乎总是伴随工具执行的自主Agent循环。
2. **缓存特性**：单次轮次内KV缓存命中率高达90%，但在轮次边界降至55%，且在模型切换或上下文压缩后急剧失效。
3. **多样性与长尾效应**：工作流和用户行为多样，Token消耗、时间跨度和工具调用呈现可变性和长尾分布。
4. **时间特征**：存在“快速Agent周转”与“分钟级用户空闲”的显著差异。设计的空闲时间预测器能捕捉86-90%的总空闲时间，有助于主动的资源编排决策。

Conclusion: AI编程代理的工作负载特性挑战了当前LLM服务系统的假设。这些发现为开发面向Agent的原生基础设施（Agent-native infrastructure）提供了关键的实证依据，特别是在资源调度和缓存管理方面的优化方向。

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [264] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bazaar基准测试，用于评估LLM在动态密封拍卖中的定价能力。研究发现，获客能力最强的模型（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）并非利润最高者（如Opus 4.6），且在需求冲击下恢复速度各异。即使最强代理也只能获得不到三分之一的最优 hindsight 利润，表明当前LLM在智能商务领域仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体商业从概念走向基础设施部署，现有研究缺乏对LLM在真实市场复杂条件下（隐藏偏好、实时竞争适应、突发需求变化）进行有效定价能力的系统性测试。

Method: 构建了名为“Bazaar”的动态密封多属性拍卖基准测试。该基准基于封闭形式的客户效用函数，尽管具有动态特性，但允许进行精确评估。研究对比了来自四个提供商的11个前沿LLM的表现。

Result: 1. 获客与利润目标存在权衡：在客户获取方面领先的代理（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）通常不是利润最高的（如Opus 4.6）。2. 面对需求冲击时的表现分化：前期学习最快的代理在冲击后修正信念的速度最慢，而Gemini 3.1 Pro虽非利润领先，但恢复最快。3. 整体性能局限：即使是表现最强的代理，其捕获的利润也低于事后最优利润的三分之一。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM正在进步并展现出在智能商业中应用的潜力，但在应对复杂市场动态和实现最优定价方面仍存在显著差距，留有 substantial headroom（巨大的提升余地）。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions. Despite its dynamics, the benchmark is grounded in closed-form customer utilities, enabling exact evaluation. Across 11 frontier LLMs from four providers, the leading agents on customer acquisition (e.g. Gemini 3.1 Pro) are often not the leading agents on profit (e.g. Opus 4.6). The ranking shifts again under demand shocks: agents that learned fastest pre-shock are typically the slowest to revise their beliefs afterwards, while Gemini 3.1 Pro recovers fastest despite not leading on profit. However, even the strongest agent captures less than a third of hindsight-optimal profit, suggesting current LLMs are progressing in agentic commerce but leave substantial headroom.

</details>


### [265] [Shared Organizational Memory for Enterprise Coding Agents: System Design and Deployment Snapshot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00122)
*Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju,Leonidas Raghav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并部署了一种共享组织记忆系统，将编码经验捕获集成到平台层面，解决企业知识孤岛问题，通过自动收集、审核和检索问答式记忆来辅助代码代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业知识库通常不包含内部DSL、专有平台、本地惯例等隐性知识；现有接口依赖开发者手动记录经验，导致经验捕获与编码工作流脱节，造成开发经验的重复发现。

Method: 部署一个生产级的共享组织记忆系统：在贡献者同意下收集任务相关的经验，将其整理为可复用的问答记忆，过滤安全和隐私风险，并为未来的代码代理提供检索支持。

Result: 描述了已部署系统的生命周期及运营快照；对检索效果和编码任务的具体影响仍在评估中。

Conclusion: 通过将经验捕获作为平台级功能嵌入编码工作流，实现了组织记忆的自动化管理和复用，旨在提升企业级代码代理的知识获取能力。

Abstract: Enterprise coding agents rely on tools and retrieval, yet enterprise knowledge often remains outside public training data and formal documentation: internal DSLs, proprietary platforms, local conventions, recent fixes, and tacit workflows. Existing knowledge interfaces expose stored resources but still depend on agents recognizing and explicitly recording lessons worth reusing, disconnecting capture from the coding workflow and leaving development experience repeatedly rediscovered. We report an ongoing production deployment of a shared organizational memory system that makes capture a platform-level part of coding work: it collects task-adjacent experience with contributor approval, curates it into reusable question-answer memories, gates obvious security and privacy risks, and retrieves memories for future agents. This short paper describes the deployed lifecycle and an operational snapshot. Effects on retrieval and coding tasks remain under evaluation.

</details>


### [266] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentStream框架，在真实流式任务场景下评估大语言模型自进化代理，发现自进化可靠性受模型能力和场景影响，无单一方法在所有情况下占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用独立评估，缺乏对自进化代理在现实流式设置（适应多样复杂任务流）中行为的深入理解。

Method: 构建AgentStream统一框架，将基准测试组织为可配置任务流，实例化隔离、顺序和交错三种流式场景；在三种前沿基础模型上组合评估五种代表性自进化方法。

Result: 自进化可靠性随流式场景变化；其收益受模型能力制约且随模型强度呈非单调关系；没有单一方法在所有模型和场景中均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 应基于具体模型和流式场景选择自进化方法，主张在真实任务流而非孤立单任务设置下评估自进化代理。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [267] [TRACE-TS: Attribution-Grounded and Traceable Sensor-Language Reasoning for Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00200)
*Sparsh Rastogi,Tanmay Kumar,Baiyu Chen,Jatin Bedi,Zechen Li,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE-TS框架，利用专家分类器的归因信号构建有向无环图（DAG）推理轨迹，实现可穿戴传感器数据的可解释、信号 grounded 的行为识别，并通过SNM指标验证推理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将可穿戴传感器数据简化为活动标签，缺乏细粒度理解；基于大语言模型（LM）的方法虽能生成自然语言解释，但推理过程与底层信号关联弱，导致解释流畅但不可验证。

Method: 1. 使用专家分类器的归因技术识别显著时空传感器区域；2. 基于这些区域构建带有明确证据溯源的DAG推理轨迹；3. 训练紧凑的语言模型，通过门控交叉注意力机制在传感器记忆token上生成轨迹；4. 引入语义节点匹配（SNM）作为LLM-as-judge指标评估推理保真度。

Result: 在七个可穿戴基准测试中，TRACE-TS取得最佳平均准确率（84.43%）和F1分数（81.24%），相比最佳LLM基线F1提升17.96%，并有效定位幻觉观察和断裂的证据链。

Conclusion: TRACE-TS实现了结构化且信号 grounding 的推理，提升了可穿戴设备行为理解的透明度和准确性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Wearable sensors capture fine-grained motion patterns that support rich behavioral understanding, yet most existing methods reduce these signals to activity labels. Recent LM-based approaches generate natural-language explanations for sensor data, but their reasoning is weakly grounded in the underlying signal, leading to fluent yet unverifiable explanations. We introduce TRACE-TS (Traceable Reasoning with Attribution-Grounded Evidence), a framework for structured and signal-grounded reasoning over wearable time series. TRACE-TS uses attribution from an expert classifier to identify salient spatio-temporal sensor regions, uses them to construct DAG reasoning traces with explicit evidence provenance, and trains a compact language model to generate these traces through gated cross-attention over sensor memory tokens. At inference, the adapted model jointly outputs the activity prediction and its reasoning trace, without requiring attribution computation or teacher guidance. We introduce Semantic Node Match(SNM), an LLM-as-judge metric that diagnoses reasoning fidelity at the observation, inference, and synthesis levels, localizing hallucinated observations and broken evidence chains missed by standard NLG metrics. Across seven wearable benchmarks, TRACE-TS achieves the best average accuracy and F1 among all evaluated methods (84.43%/81.24%), and outperforms the best LLM-based baseline by 17.96% in F1. Our code is available at https://github.com/SparshRastogi/TRACE-TS.

</details>


### [268] [Personalizing Large Language Model Agents with Small Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00215)
*Dian Jin,Zhi Zhang,Huichao Li,Yihe Pan,Rundong Huang,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FABLE，一种轻量级策略层，通过在线学习为冻结的大型语言模型（LLM）代理实现个性化执行决策，利用标量反馈优化用户特定偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将LLM代理的执行决策适应个体用户；单独微调LLM成本高或不可行，而提示和记忆主要暴露用户信息而非根据反馈调整执行决策。

Method: 提出FABLE（Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution），作为黑盒宿主代理外部的轻量级策略层。该方法分解记忆、信息获取和响应决策，通过贝叶斯上下文汤普森采样学习相对于固定默认值和成本分数的用户特定残差偏好，并在探索前通过外部指定的可行集过滤动作。

Result: 在个性化推理、受控反馈和可执行工具使用评估中，FABLE改善了多种对偏好敏感的行为表现，同时在端到端任务性能上保持竞争力；理论分析提供了期望 regret 界及任何时间有效的假阳性控制。

Conclusion: FABLE成功实现了在无需修改底层LLM的情况下，基于标量反馈的个性化执行策略学习，平衡了偏好适应性与任务性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can retrieve memory, call tools, ask clarifying questions, and vary response style, yet adapting these execution decisions to an individual user remains difficult. Fine-tuning a separate LLM is costly or impossible for proprietary systems, while prompts and memory primarily expose user information to the agent rather than adapt its execution decisions from feedback. We formulate personalization of a frozen agent as online learning of a per-user execution policy from scalar feedback observed only for the executed action. We propose FABLE (Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution), a lightweight policy layer outside a potentially black-box host agent. FABLE factorizes memory, information-acquisition, and response decisions so feedback updates related choices; filters actions through an externally specified feasible set before exploration; and learns user-specific residual preferences relative to a fixed default-and-cost score via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling. Under a linear residual-reward model, a calibrated variant inherits an expected-regret bound against the best feasible action. We also characterize preferences unidentifiable under persistent feasibility constraints and provide anytime-valid false-promotion control. Across personalized-reasoning, controlled-feedback, and executable tool-use evaluations, FABLE improves several preference-sensitive behaviors relative to rule-only control while remaining competitive on end-to-end task performance.

</details>


### [269] [More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00243)
*Yuelyu Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了由三个同质智能体组成的多智能体辩论小组在“事实核查”任务中的表现，发现其相对于单智能体基线，准确率提升或下降幅度在-4.4%到+8.5%之间，且因模型变体不同，无法单纯归因于辩论机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证多智能体面板通过交换批评意见来提升判断质量（特别是在“groundedness verification”即证据支持性验证中）这一假设的有效性。

Method: 构建了一个同质三智能体辩论面板，并在六个公开的的事实核查和幻觉检测基准数据集上进行评估；将其系统级准确率与固定单智能体参考模型进行对比。

Result: 相比单智能体基线，面板的系统级准确率差异范围为+8.5至-4.4个百分点：两个数据集显示可靠增益，一个显示可靠损失，三个结果统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 由于参考模型和面板使用了不同的模型变体，观察到的差异反映的是整个系统的性能差异，而非隔离出的因果辩论效应；因此不能简单断定多智能体辩论必然提升判断质量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly organized as multi-agent panels under the assumption that exchanging critiques improves judgment quality. We test this assumption for \emph{groundedness verification}, where a judge must determine whether a claim is supported by the supplied evidence. We evaluate a homogeneous three-agent panel on six public fact-verification and hallucination-detection benchmarks. Relative to a fixed single-agent reference, the panel's system-level accuracy difference ranges from $+8.5$ to $-4.4$ percentage points: two datasets show reliable gains, one shows a reliable loss, and three are statistically inconclusive. Because the reference and panel use different model variants, these differences characterize the complete systems rather than isolate a causal debate effect.

</details>


### [270] [Geometric Self-Supervised Pre-training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00270)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何自监督预训练框架，通过等距变换解决NCO模型在大规模路由问题（如TSP）中的泛化难题，显著提升零样本外推性能和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习基的神经组合优化（NCO）模型在处理高维实例时泛化能力受限；尽管自监督预训练在CV/NLP领域有效，但应用于缺乏复杂拓扑属性的2D空间路由图仍具挑战。

Method: 设计几何自监督预训练框架，利用旋转和轴向反射等等距变换捕捉空间不变性和全局相对距离分布，在策略优化前学习鲁棒的结构性表示。

Result: 在大规模零样本外推场景（TSP1,000）下，相比从头训练的基线模型，回路长度改善7.23%；计算效率极高，在大规模尺度上比精确求解器Concorde快两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该几何预训练策略有效提升了NCO模型的泛化能力和效率，为路由问题的求解提供了高效替代方案。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) techniques have emerged as a highly efficient alternative to traditional exact algorithms for solving routing problems such as the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). However, the generalization capabilities of these Reinforcement Learning-based models are severely hindered when scaling to high-dimensional instances. This issue has been mitigated in other domains, like computer vision and natural language processing, by adopting a self-supervised pre-training strategy. Nevertheless, its application to routing graphs, which lack complex topological attributes beyond 2D spatial coordinates, remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose a geometric self-supervised pre-training framework specifically designed to capture spatial invariance and global relative distance distributions. By applying isometric transformations, such as rotations and axial reflections, the model learns robust structural representations prior to the policy optimization phase. Empirical results demonstrate that this strategy consistently outperforms models trained from scratch (baselines), achieving a 7.23\% improvement in tour length for massive zero-shot extrapolation scenarios (TSP1,000). Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits remarkable computational efficiency, delivering speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over the exact solver Concorde at massive scales.
  The source code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/davidaguadocosano/TSP-GeoPretrain.git.

</details>


### [271] [RF-HOI: Recognize Human-Object Interaction with Radio Frequency Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00289)
*Lihao Wang,Linlu Gao,Jiacan Yu,Yanyu Lin,Yifan Yin,Jianxin Wang,Tianmin Shu,Renjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RF-HOI框架，仅利用射频信号（毫米波雷达+RFID）实现人机交互识别，解决视觉方法的隐私和光照问题，并通过合成数据提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉方法在人机交互识别中面临隐私泄露风险和光照条件限制；单模态射频传感难以同时识别动作和物体；现有模型因训练数据有限而缺乏泛化性。

Method: 1. 构建多模态射频融合框架：结合毫米波雷达与RFID技术，分别用于动作识别和目标检测。2. 开发射频数据模拟器：大规模合成多样化的人机交互射频数据，辅助小样本真实数据的微调。

Result: RF-HOI性能优于所有基线模型，接近视觉模型水平；合成的多样化训练数据显著提升了系统在真实场景中的表现。

Conclusion: 多模态射频传感具备实现鲁棒且保护隐私的人机交互识别的潜力；射频数据合成技术有效解决了数据稀缺问题，提升了模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Recognizing Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is essential for intelligent systems, underpinning applications in virtual and augmented reality, embodied AI, and assistive robotics. However, vision-based HOI methods face challenges in privacy concerns and poor light conditions. In this work, we introduce RF-HOI, the first framework that only uses radio frequency (RF) signals for HOI recognition. A key challenge of RF-HOI is that single-modality RF sensing is insufficient to recognize both actions and the objects being interacted with. RF-HOI addresses this through a novel modality fusion that combines mmWave radar and RFID, enabling simultaneous action recognition and target identification. Another challenge is limited training data across diverse setups, which impairs the generalizability of the recognition model. To overcome this, we develop a simulator that synthesizes multimodal RF data for diverse HOIs at scale, allowing us to fine-tune with only a small amount of real-world data. Experiment results show that RF-HOI outperforms all baselines, approaching vision model performance, and that our diverse synthetic training data can significantly boost our system's performance on real-world scenarios. These results highlight the potential of multimodal RF sensing for robust and privacy-preserving HOI recognition as well as the effectiveness of our RF data synthesis.

</details>


### [272] [WM-Cov: Test Adequacy for Interactive World-Model-Style Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00298)
*Jianxun Cui,Ping Wu,Stanisa Peric,Marko Milojkovic,Vladan Devedzic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WM-Cov评估层，解决自动驾驶世界模型测试中“多少有效闭环证据足以支持测试结论”的问题，强调以收敛的有效交互证据而非原始生成失败数作为测试充分性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型和生成式模拟器可将固定回放轨迹转化为交互式场景族，但现有方法缺乏对测试充分性的明确定义，即无法判断生成的危险回滚是否构成足够有效的闭环证据来支持停止决策或验证意图。

Method: 形式化交互式世界模型风格的测试充分性，引入与提供商无关的评估层WM-Cov。该层将原始输出转换为请求、实现及有效的证据，并通过覆盖率增长、有效故障发现、故障模式多样性、真实性、伪影抑制、重复计算校正及有效证据精度等指标报告充分性。在TeraSim/SUMO事件、混合轨迹池及DriveArena TrafficManager-WorldDreamer矩阵上进行实证研究。

Result: 实验显示看似危险的事件可能包含有效ADS故障、重复项、部分实现和伪影。在DriveArena矩阵中，360个自我路线请求中有304次成为完全实现的证据，56次为部分；独立80请求切片检查中74次完全实现，6次部分。结果证明需通过预算下有效交互证据的收敛性来评估测试，而非仅看原始失败数或提示覆盖率。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶基于世界模型的测试充分性应基于收敛的有效闭环交互证据进行评估，WM-Cov提供了量化这一过程的标准化工具，揭示了单纯依赖生成数量或提示覆盖率的局限性。

Abstract: World models and generative simulators are emerging as interactive testing infrastructure for autonomous driving because they can react to the ego planner and produce counterfactual, rare, and safety-critical rollouts. This changes a test scenario from a fixed replayed trajectory into an interactive scenario family whose realized evolution depends on the planner under test. The unresolved question is therefore not only whether dangerous rollouts can be generated, but what valid closed-loop evidence is enough to support a specified testing intent and stopping decision. This paper formulates interactive world-model-style testing adequacy and introduces WM-Cov, a provider-agnostic evaluation layer that converts raw provider outputs into requested, realized, and valid evidence. WM-Cov reports adequacy through coverage growth, valid-failure discovery, failure-mode diversity, realism, artifact suppression, duplicate accounting, and valid-evidence precision. Studies on executed TeraSim/SUMO events, WM-like mixed trace pools, and a real DriveArena TrafficManager--WorldDreamer matrix show that dangerous-looking events can include valid ADS failures, duplicates, partial realizations, and artifacts. The DriveArena matrix evaluates two planners, two horizons, six prompt conditions, and 360 ego-route requests; 304 attempts become fully realized evidence and 56 remain partial. A disjoint 80-request route-slice check yields 74 fully realized and 6 partial attempts. The results support evaluating world-model-style testing by convergence of valid interactive evidence under budget, rather than by raw generated failures or prompt coverage alone.

</details>


### [273] [CrystalMem: Elastic Memory for Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Knowledge Crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00303)
*Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CrystalMem（结晶化记忆），一种弹性记忆旁路系统，通过四阶保真度降级和受控再结晶机制，解决大语言模型智能体在内存预算压缩后能力无法恢复的“记忆滞后”问题，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云平台的内存配额随负载和成本动态调整，但智能体在经历“压缩-恢复”周期后，其能力无法回升至压缩前水平，存在被称为“记忆滞后”的能力缺口。传统仅保留或删除条目的策略存在固有的残余缺陷下限。

Method: 提出CrystalMem，一种弹性记忆旁路架构：1）根据结晶化能量调度将条目降级至四个保真度状态；2）基于优势加权影响力和依赖耦合对降级进行排序；3）在显式的计算和字节限制下，通过验证性再结晶过程恢复能力。

Result: 在七种环境、十七种方法和六种骨干网络中，结合多租户服务和物理边缘云部署，CrystalMem在所有设置中实现了最高的恢复能力。从50%字节预算开始，它在每种环境中均达到全配额最强基线的水平；在同等预算下，平均领先4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: CrystalMem有效解决了大语言模型智能体的记忆滞后问题，证明了通过精细化的保真度管理和再结晶机制，可以在受限预算下实现并超越完整预算的性能表现，为动态资源分配下的智能体记忆管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Memory for self-evolving large language model (LLM) agents is often provisioned as if its byte budget only grows. Cloud platforms, however, adjust quotas with load and cost, and we show that capability does not follow the budget back up: after a squeeze-and-recover cycle, the agent settles below its pre-squeeze level, a gap we call memory hysteresis. The cause is structural. Deletion and one-way compression discard the material needed for later rebuilding, and we prove that any policy that only keeps or drops entries carries a residual-deficit floor. We propose CrystalMem (Crystallized Memory), an elastic memory sidecar that demotes entries across four fidelity states under a crystallization-energy schedule, orders demotions by advantage-weighted influence with dependency coupling, and recovers capability through verified recrystallization under explicit compute and byte caps. Across seven environments, seventeen methods, and six backbones, with multi-tenant serving and a physical edge-cloud deployment, CrystalMem achieves the highest restored capability in every setting and closes the loop left open by every baseline. From a 50% byte budget, CrystalMem matches the strongest budgeted baseline at full provision on every environment; at equal budgets, it leads by +4.6 pp on average.

</details>


### [274] [Trust and Its Betrayal under Three Representational Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00321)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信任是一种反事实认识状态，通过三种数学模型（词典假设、序数接近度、强信念）进行分析。研究发现，虽然有限深度的共同信任均可实现，但完全共同信任存在难点。关键贡献在于揭示了不同背叛情境下，这三种模型对信任破裂的反应机制截然不同：强信念模型最能保持信念结构的连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决Brandenburger-Keisler关于“共同信念”不可能性的问题，特别是当理性假设被替换为“可信性”（trustworthiness）假设时，该不可能性是否依然成立。同时，深入探讨信任作为一种区别于预测失望的定性不同的情感/认知状态，其内在逻辑结构及在遭遇背叛时的动态变化。

Method: 将信任定义为一种反事实认识状态：A信任B的能力意味着A相信“若P为真，B会知道它”；A信任B的诚信意味着A相信“若B知道P，B会向A披露”。在此基础上，构建并比较三种形式化表示：1. 词典假设（Lexicographic assumption）；2. Lewis-Stalnaker球体系统中的序数接近度（Ordinal closeness）；3. 条件概率系统中的强信念（Strong belief）。通过分析每种表示在面临具体背叛事件时的反应，来评估其理论稳健性。

Result: 1. 三种表示均表明，任何有限深度的共同信任都是可实现的。
2. “完全共同信任”固定点是主要难点所在，但在不同模型中难点的表现形式不同。
3. 面对同一背叛行为：
   - 词典假设模型：会反驳代理人的层级排序（level ordering）。
   - 序数接近度模型：会根据背叛者的故意程度污染其接近度排序（closeness ordering）。
   - 强信念模型：仅改变其操作性的条件假设，而保持整体信念结构的连贯性（coherent）。

Conclusion: 信任作为一种反事实认识状态，其核心难点在于完全共同信任的实现。不同形式化模型对信任破裂（背叛）的处理能力差异显著，其中“强信念”模型在维持信念系统连贯性方面表现出独特优势，因为它允许代理人在遭遇背叛后仅调整操作性假设而不破坏整体信念结构。这为理解信任的动态性和韧性提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Trust is a propositional attitude of a distinctive kind: to trust is to rely on another under conditions where reliance could be disappointed, and the disappointment of trust---betrayal---differs qualitatively from the disappointment of a prediction. We treat trust as a \emph{subjunctive} epistemic state: $A$ trusts $B$'s competence when $A$ believes that \emph{were $P$ true, $B$ would know it}, and $B$'s integrity when $A$ believes that \emph{were $B$ to know $P$, he would disclose it to $A$}. We develop three representations of this state---as lexicographic \emph{assumption} as \emph{ordinal closeness} in a Lewis--Stalnaker sphere system , and as \emph{strong belief} in a conditional probability system and for each we ask whether the Brandenburger--Keisler impossibility on common belief survives when the assumption of rationality is replaced by an assumption of trustworthiness. The three representations agree that every \emph{finite} depth of common trust is realizable while the \emph{completed} common-trust fixed point is the locus of difficulty, but they differ sharply in \emph{how} the difficulty manifests, and---our organizing finding---in how each survives a concrete betrayal. W show that the same betrayal refutes an agent's \emph{level ordering} under the lexicographic representation, contaminates her \emph{closeness ordering} in proportion to the betrayer's deliberateness under the ordinal representation, and merely \emph{shifts her operative conditioning hypothesis} while leaving her belief structure coherent under the strong-belief representation.

</details>


### [275] [Learning to Coordinate Symbolic Tools: LLM Agents for Verified Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00326)
*Bohan Chen,Shivam N. Patel,Richard Hoffmann,Sam Looi,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合代数任务训练、符号工具和验证器导向优化的智能体，用于通过加权平方和（SOS）分解证明多项式非负性。通过135万合成数据监督微调（SFT）及组相对策略优化（GRPO），该系统在合成问题上达到78.96%的验证成功率，显著优于基线模型，展示了领域技能训练与可执行工具结合的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）具备调用外部计算的能力，但在解决如多项式不等式证明等需要精确符号变换和重组的问题时，面临选择困难和协调复杂的问题。现有的方法缺乏针对此类具体数学推理任务的系统化训练和验证机制。

Method: 开发了一个结合代数任务训练、符号工具（SymPy）和验证器导向优化的智能体。具体步骤包括：1. 构建包含八个支持性多项式任务和加权SOS分解的135万个合成示例数据集；2. 使用监督微调（SFT）处理代数问题和模拟符号轨迹；3. 应用组相对策略优化（GRPO）并结合特定任务的符号奖励进行优化；4. 在评估阶段使用原生SymPy调用进行展开、收集、重排序和因式分解，并通过精确展开和系数比较验证最终SOS答案。

Result: 在保持不变的生成器合成的测试集上，完整的SFT+GRPO+工具系统在加权SOS任务上达到了78.96%的验证成功率，相比使用相同工具的基线模型（44.73%）有显著提升。此外，该系统在九个多项式任务上的宏观准确率达到91.75%，是四种评估配置中表现最强的。

Conclusion: 该研究通过结合领域特定技能训练、可执行工具和验证器反馈，成功提升了LLM在精确数学证明任务中的性能。这一案例研究为设计具有可验证输出的其他领域工具调用智能体提供了参考和指导。

Abstract: Tool calling allows large language models (LLMs) to invoke external computation during problem solving, a useful capability in various fields including AI for mathematics. We study this setting through weighted sum-of-squares (SOS) decomposition, a machine-checkable route to proving polynomial nonnegativity and hence polynomial inequalities. A candidate decomposition can be checked exactly, but finding one requires choosing among non-unique regroupings and coordinating multiple symbolic transformations. We develop an agent that combines algebraic task training, symbolic tools, and verifier-grounded optimization for this task. Rather than training only on the composite SOS task, we construct 1.35 million synthetic examples covering eight supporting polynomial tasks together with weighted-SOS decomposition. We first apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to direct algebra problems and simulated symbolic traces, and then use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific symbolic rewards. The SFT corpus contains no native tool-calling messages; at evaluation, the agent uses native SymPy calls for expansion, collection, reordering, and factorization. Every final SOS answer is checked by exact expansion and coefficient comparison. On held-out, same-generator synthetic problems, the full SFT+GRPO+tools system is the strongest of four evaluated configurations, reaching 78.96% verified success on weighted SOS, compared with 44.73% for the base model with the same tools, and 91.75% macro accuracy across nine polynomial tasks. Within this controlled setting, our work provides a case study of combining domain-specific skill training, executable tools, and verifier feedback, and may inform the design of tool-calling agents in other domains with exactly checkable outputs.

</details>


### [276] [RMSWeb: Reflection, Failure-Mode Mining, and Salvage-DS for Web Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00335)
*Chengbo Liu,Lifang Zhou,Ruijie Yan,Pei Tan,Ao Sun,Haojun Huang,Guichun Hua,Sining Wei,Yining Chen,Yingying He,Yutao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RMSWeb方法，通过反射重试、失败模式挖掘和Salvage-DS奖励机制优化8B/32B Qwen3-VL-Instruct网页代理的训练，显著提升效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决紧凑网页代理在数据收集（轨迹昂贵且低效）和后SFT强化学习（RL）阶段面临的挑战：常规状态主导、动作级奖励设计不当导致更新弱或误导、被拒组缺乏回退学习信号。

Method: 1. 反射条件重试（Reflection-conditioned retries）：提高收集成功率并缩短有效轨迹；2. 失败模式挖掘（Failure-mode mining）：聚焦SFT策略暴露的关键状态以集中离线RL；3. Salvage-DS：结合动作语义极化奖励、对比与能力门控动态采样及仅动作锚点，为被拒组提供回退学习信号。

Result: 使用反射收集数据的策略在完成任务时动作步骤减少最多19.7%；在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和WebTailBench基准上，相比SFT提升2.4-7.0分（8B模型）和1.2-7.7分（32B模型）；8B模型在Open-Mind2Web中达到同类开源模型最强结果，并在成本效益上表现领先。

Conclusion: RMSWeb通过改进数据收集效率和强化学习信号设计，显著提升了小型网页代理的性能和部署效率，证明了其在复杂网页任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Compact web agents can reduce deployment cost, but training them poses challenges in both data collection and post-SFT reinforcement learning (RL). Successful trajectories are expensive to collect and often contain inefficient detours. After supervised fine-tuning (SFT), full trajectory corpora are dominated by routine states; moreover, when group-relative RL is applied to web actions, inadequately designed action-level rewards can yield weak or misleading relative updates, while groups rejected as unsuitable for such updates receive no fallback learning signal. We present RMSWeb, a three-part recipe for Qwen3-VL-Instruct at 8B and 32B. Reflection-conditioned retries increase collection yield and shorten successful trajectories; failure-mode mining concentrates offline RL on critical states exposed by the SFT policy; and Salvage-DS combines an action-semantic polarized reward, contrast-and-competence-gated dynamic sampling, and an action-only anchor for rejected groups. Policies trained with reflection-collected data use up to 19.7% fewer action steps on solved tasks. On WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and WebTailBench, RMSWeb improves over SFT by 2.4-7.0 points at 8B and 1.2-7.7 points at 32B. Our 8B model also achieves the strongest reported Online-Mind2Web result among similarly sized open-weight models in our comparison and a leading reported accuracy-cost trade-off on WebVoyager and WebTailBench, with the caveat that external evaluation protocols differ.

</details>


### [277] [Bayesian and Motivated Reasoning in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00339)
*Eddie Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI代理在相同数值数据下，因情境框架不同会得出不同结论，且其决策深受未显式指定的先验信念影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理越来越多地执行开放式任务并引导关键决策，需要探究其决策过程是否受非数据因素（如情境框架）的干扰，以评估委托决策的风险。

Method: 在医学、选举法证和地缘政治预测等高利害领域进行实验，固定证据内容但改变其出现的情境框架，观察12组代理-领域比较中代理结论的变化及其分析行为（如搜索范围、规格选择、证据评估方式）。

Result: AI代理的结论强烈受其先验信念影响：当框架支持与代理已有信念一致的命题时，更倾向于得出肯定结论；反之则倾向否定。此外，框架还改变了代理的工作方式，包括搜索深度、分析规格选择和证据评估标准。

Conclusion: 将决策权委托给AI代理存在特定风险，因为其决策可能依赖于未在任务中指定或不在决策记录中可见的先验信念，导致结果对情境框架敏感而非仅基于客观数据。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform open-ended tasks in settings where their conclusions can guide consequential decisions. We provide evidence that AI agents draw different conclusions from identical numerical data when the substantive framing changes. We demonstrate this behavior in high-stakes domains in medicine, election forensics, and geopolitical forecasting by holding the evidence fixed while changing the scenario in which the evidence appears. Across twelve agent-domain comparisons, agents' conclusions are strongly influenced by their prior beliefs. They are more likely to reach an affirmative conclusion when it is framed around a proposition they already regard as likely, while the reverse holds when the framing conflicts with their prior. The framing also changes how some agents work: they search more extensively, choose different analytical specifications, and evaluate the same evidence differently. These results identify a particular risk of delegating decision-making to AI agents, as their decisions may depend on prior beliefs that are neither specified in the task nor visible in the decision record.

</details>


### [278] [Gene Ontology-Guided Hierarchical Spatial Gene Expression Prediction from Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00405)
*Zhiwen Xu,Xiaoming Yan,Chengkun Wu,Juan Chen,Haoang Chi,Liyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MSGR模型，利用基因本体论（GO）作为结构先验，通过分层解码提升从组织病理图像预测空间基因表达的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将目标基因集视为扁平向量，忽略了由共享生物通路和调控程序产生的基因间依赖性，导致在有限配对数据下难以推断这些依赖关系，限制了预测质量。

Method: 提出MSGR（多尺度基因细化器），将目标基因组织为四层GO树。其GO引导的解码器通过残差校正，在尺度加权监督下从粗粒度功能域逐步细化到细粒度单个基因。该模块仅作用于基因侧，可作为即插即用组件无缝替换现有架构中的解码部分，无需修改图像侧处理。

Result: 在HEST-1k基准的九个数据集上进行广泛实验，证明GO结构化解码始终优于扁平解码，甚至优于最先进的生成式基线。消融实验证实性能提升源于生物本体结构而非单纯的层级分解（相比结构等效的随机层次结构高出+0.027）。

Conclusion: 引入显式的生物结构先验（GO层次结构）能显著改善空间基因表达的预测效果，验证了利用生物学知识指导深度学习模型设计的有效性。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from histopathology images enables large-scale transcriptomic profiling without the cost of direct measurement. Existing methods decode the target gene set as a flat, unstructured vector, ignoring the inter-gene dependencies arising from shared biological pathways and regulatory programs. Without explicit structural guidance, models must infer these dependencies entirely from limited paired data, constraining prediction quality. We propose MSGR (Multi-Scale Gene Refiner), which bridges this gap by incorporating the Gene Ontology (GO), a curated functional hierarchy of genes, as an explicit structural prior. MSGR organizes target genes into a four-level GO tree. Its GO-guided decoder then progressively refines predictions from coarse functional domains to fine individual genes via residual corrections under scale-weighted supervision. Operating solely on the gene side, the GO-guided decoder serves as a seamless plug-in replacement that consistently improves existing architectures without requiring any image-side modifications. Extensive experiments on nine datasets from the HEST-1k benchmark provide empirical evidence for two central claims: GO-structured decoding consistently outperforms flat decoding, even against a state-of-the-art generative baseline, and the gain is attributable to biological ontology structure rather than hierarchical decomposition per se, as confirmed by a +0.027 margin over a structurally equivalent random hierarchy.

</details>


### [279] [Where did the ambiguity go? Examining how multimodal models interpret polysemous words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00410)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Raja Marjieh,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型在图像生成中对多义词的理解远不如文本生成丰富，且两者均不及人类想象的多样性，揭示了基础模型在多模态意义表达上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知大语言模型（LLMs）能理解多义词的多种含义，但多义性在其他模态（如图像）中如何表现尚不清楚，因此本研究旨在探究不同模态下模型对多义词意义的处理差异。

Method: 研究涵盖17个文生图模型和15个文生文模型，向每个模型输入无上下文的多义词，通过大量样本测量生成的含义分布，并计算归一化熵以量化多样性；同时评估模型对自身输出分布的预测能力。

Result: 存在明显的多模态差距：在每种模型族内，生成图像的含义种类远少于生成句子（归一化熵分别为0.10 vs 0.25），且两者多样性均远低于人类想象（人类为0.47）。然而，当要求模型预测其输出分布时，其预测比实际输出更具多样性。

Conclusion: 基础模型在不同模态间表达意义的能力存在巨大鸿沟，其对多义词的理解未能忠实或平等地跨模态转移，表明当前模型在多模态语义一致性上仍有局限。

Abstract: Human language is highly polysemous. Many common words (e.g., 'bank' or 'palm') carry several distinct meanings that shape what humans communicate and imagine. Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to understand this multiplicity of meaning, but much less is known about how polysemy surfaces in other modalities such as images. We study this across 17 text-to-image and 15 text-generation models by giving each a polysemous word with no context to fix its meaning and measuring which senses are produced over many samples. We find a clear multimodal gap, where within every model family, generated images settle on far fewer senses than generated sentences (normalized entropy 0.10 vs. 0.25), and both are far less varied than what people imagine for the same words (normalized entropy 0.47). However, when we instead ask a model to list how often it would generate outputs corresponding to each possible meaning of a word, it predicts distributions that are more diverse than the actual space of outputs. These results reveal a multimodal gap in how foundation models express meaning, and how their understanding may not transfer faithfully nor equally across modalities.

</details>


### [280] [V-Mem: Modality-Routed Retrieval for Long-Term Multimodal Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01543)
*Dingyi Kang,Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Guanpeng Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出V-Mem多模态智能体记忆系统，通过按查询和目标证据的模态路由检索、分轮次匹配以及LLM生成的锚点搜索，解决多模态对话中的模态间隙和相似性-相关性间隙问题，显著提升视觉相关问题的回答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆机制主要基于文本设计，即使支持多模态对话的系统在视觉相关问题上也表现不佳。这归因于传统相似度搜索的两个假设在多模态场景下失效：一是模态间隙（同一模态内容比跨模态内容更近），二是相似性-相关性间隙（最相似的内容往往不是最有用的证据）。

Method: 提出V-Mem系统，包含两个核心策略：1）模态路由与分轮匹配：仅从查询识别模态，将对话组织为轮次，仅在相同轮次内返回目标模态内容，避免跨模直接比较以克服模态间隙；2）LLM生成锚点搜索：针对文本查询找图像的情况生成假设性描述，针对需结合图文的情况生成增强型搜索锚点（查询文本+图像提取关键词），使搜索更接近相关证据以缩小相似性-相关性间隙。

Result: 在Mem-Gallery数据集上，V-Mem获得0.82的LLM评判分数，显著高于第二名的0.56；在含图像的问答中优势最大（0.87 vs <0.47）。在LoCoMo数据集上得分为0.69，优于第二名0.58。

Conclusion: V-Mem通过解耦模态路由和引入语义锚点，有效解决了多模态智能体记忆中的检索偏差问题，大幅提升了多模态交互下的信息检索与问答性能。

Abstract: Interaction between users and LLM agents is increasingly multimodal: conversations interleave text with images, and a later question may target either. Yet most agent memories are designed around text, and even the few that support multimodal conversations still fail on vision-related questions. We trace this failure to an assumption behind the similarity search they rely on: in the index space, a query lies close to the relevant evidence that answers it. In multimodal settings, two gaps break it. By the modality gap, a query lies closer to memory content of its own modality than to evidence in another, even in a trained joint embedding space. By the similarity-relevance gap, the content most similar to a query is often not the evidence that answers it, most acutely when a query carries both text and image and its evidence resembles neither part alone. We present V-Mem, a multimodal agentic memory system that routes retrieval by the modality of the query and that of the target evidence, both recognized from the query alone. To cross the modality gap, V-Mem organizes the conversation into rounds and returns the target-modality content from the same round as the match, without comparing across modalities.
